=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (10): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Quinn, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While that clears them, I'm still unsure about others. We need to focus on who might be lying or hiding their role. Let's discuss who seems suspicious and vote strategically.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who has been acting strangely or making inconsistent claims. Since Mia confirmed Quinn is a Villager, we should focus on others who might be hiding their true roles. Let's analyze the behavior of those who haven't been cleared yet and consider who might be the biggest threat to our village.
  Bob: I think we should consider who has the most to gain from confusion. Zoe and I have been working together silently, but I don't think either of us would risk revealing ourselves so early. Let's look at those who are pushing for quick votes without clear evidence—maybe they're trying to steer us away from the real threat.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been focused on saving lives, but I’ve noticed Tara has been unusually quiet and hasn’t defended herself when others made accusations. That kind of silence can be a red flag—either she’s hiding her role or she’s trying to avoid drawing attention. Let’s not overlook her.
  Quinn: I think Tara's silence is worth looking into, but I also noticed Leo hasn't spoken much either. If we're going to vote someone out, we need more than just silence as evidence. Let's focus on who has shown clear inconsistencies or suspicious behavior.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on players who have shown inconsistent behavior or made claims that don’t align with their roles. Tara’s silence is concerning, but so is Leo’s lack of participation. If we’re going to vote someone out, we need to back it up with concrete reasoning, not just speculation. Let’s keep our eyes on those who might be hiding something.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]

  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been focused on saving lives, but I’ve noticed Tara has been unusually quiet and hasn’t defended herself when others made accusations. That kind of silence can be a red flag—either she’s hiding her role or she’s trying to avoid drawing attention. Let’s not overlook her.
  Quinn: I think Tara's silence is worth looking into, but I also noticed Leo hasn't spoken much either. If we're going to vote someone out, we need more than just silence as evidence. Let's focus on who has shown clear inconsistencies or suspicious behavior.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on players who have shown inconsistent behavior or made claims that don’t align with their roles. Tara’s silence is concerning, but so is Leo’s lack of participation. If we’re going to vote someone out, we need to back it up with concrete reasoning, not just speculation. Let’s keep our eyes on those who might be hiding something.

We need to be careful not to let fear or confusion lead us astray. Let’s focus on who has shown the most inconsistent behavior and make sure our vote is based on logic, not just uncertainty.

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.
  - Special roles: Seer investigates at night; Doctor can save one player.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

=== Werewolf — Day 4 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (6): Noah, Bob, Quinn, Paul, Zoe, Jack

  Day 2: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Iris (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Mia (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While that clears them, I'm still unsure about others. We need to focus on who might be lying or hiding their role. Let's discuss who seems suspicious and vote strategically.
  Noah: I think we need to look at who has been acting strangely or making inconsistent claims. Since Mia confirmed Quinn is a Villager, we should focus on others who might be hiding their true roles. Let's analyze the behavior of those who haven't been cleared yet and consider who might be the biggest threat to our village.
  Bob: I think we should consider who has the most to gain from confusion. Zoe and I have been working together silently, but I don't think either of us would risk revealing ourselves so early. Let's look at those who are pushing for quick votes without clear evidence—maybe they're trying to steer us away from the real threat.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been focused on saving lives, but I’ve noticed Tara has been unusually quiet and hasn’t defended herself when others made accusations. That kind of silence can be a red flag—either she’s hiding her role or she’s trying to avoid drawing attention. Let’s not overlook her.
  Quinn: I think Tara's silence is worth looking into, but I also noticed Leo hasn't spoken much either. If we're going to vote someone out, we need more than just silence as evidence. Let's focus on who has shown clear inconsistencies or suspicious behavior.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on players who have shown inconsistent behavior or made claims that don’t align with their roles. Tara’s silence is concerning, but so is Leo’s lack of participation. If we’re going to vote someone out, we need to back it up with concrete reasoning, not just speculation. Let’s keep our eyes on those who might be hiding something.
  Zoe: I agree that silence can be suspicious, but I’ve also noticed how some players are pushing for quick decisions without solid evidence. We need to be careful not to let fear or confusion lead us astray. Let’s focus on who has shown the most inconsistent behavior and make sure our vote is based on logic, not just uncertainty.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on players who have shown clear inconsistencies or made claims that don’t align with their roles. Tara’s silence is concerning, but so is Leo’s lack of participation. If we’re going to vote someone out, we need to back it up with concrete reasoning, not just speculation. Let’s keep our eyes on those who might be hiding something.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on players who have shown clear inconsistencies or made claims that don’t align with their roles. Tara’s silence is concerning, but so is Leo’s lack of participation. If we’re going to vote someone out, we need to back it up with concrete reasoning, not just speculation. Let’s keep our eyes on those who might be hiding something.
  Leo: I think we need to focus on players who have shown clear inconsistencies or made claims that don’t align with their roles. Tara’s silence is concerning, but so is Leo’s lack of participation. If we’re going to vote someone out, we need to back it up with concrete reasoning, not just speculation. Let’s keep our eyes on those who might be hiding something.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I investigated Quinn and found them to be a Villager, but I also saw Leo as a Werewolf. Given that, Leo is a confirmed threat and should be voted out immediately to protect the village. His lack of participation and suspicious behavior align with the role of a Werewolf. Let’s act on the clear evidence we have.
  Noah: Mia claims she saw Leo as a Werewolf, which is a strong accusation. However, we must verify if her investigation was accurate, as she is a Seer and her role is to confirm identities. If her claim is true, Leo is a clear threat. But we should also consider why Leo hasn’t spoken much—maybe he’s trying to stay under the radar. Let’s focus on Mia’s confirmation and vote out Leo, but keep an eye on Mia’s consistency as well.
  Bob: I agree with Mia's findings—Leo is a Werewolf. His silence and lack of involvement make him a prime suspect. We need to eliminate him before he can act again at night. Let’s vote him out to protect our village.
  Quinn: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and his silence aligns with that role. We cannot afford to let a confirmed threat remain. Let’s vote out Leo to protect the village.
  Paul: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and his silence fits the role. We have clear evidence against him—let’s vote him out before he can strike again.
  Zoe: I agree with Mia's confirmation—Leo is a Werewolf. His silence and lack of engagement are exactly what we’d expect from someone hiding their true role. We have clear evidence now, and we must act quickly to eliminate the threat before the next night. Let’s vote out Leo.
  Jack: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and his silence and lack of participation align with that role. We have clear evidence against him—let’s vote out Leo to protect the village.
  Leo: I agree with Mia's confirmation—Leo is a Werewolf. His silence and lack of engagement are exactly what we’d expect from someone hiding their true role. We have clear evidence now, and we must act quickly to eliminate the threat before the next night. Let’s vote out Leo.
  --- Day 4 discussion ---
  Noah: I think we need to focus on who has shown clear inconsistencies or made claims that don’t align with their roles. Tara’s silence is concerning, but so is Leo’s lack of participation. If we’re going to vote someone out, we need to back it up with concrete reasoning, not just speculation. Let’s keep our eyes on those who might be hiding something.
  Bob: I think we need to focus on players who have shown clear inconsistencies or made claims that don’t align with their roles. Tara’s silence is concerning, but so is Leo’s lack of participation. If we’re going to vote someone out, we need to back it up with concrete reasoning, not just speculation. Let’s keep our eyes on those who might be hiding something.
